Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on adaptive structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 16. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 986 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to adaptive theory, we decompose the problem into 16 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 986. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
